{
  "parentUuid": "143b2ab5-056d-4072-84f1-e8b935ce84f2",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. SSH 连接 westd-39279\n2. 运行 `nvidia-smi` 查看两张 GPU 的进程\n3. 对 cuda:0 上的进程(PID 58051 或当前占用进程) 运行:\n   - `ps -p &lt;PID> -o pid,ppid,cmd,etime,user --no-headers`\n   - `ls -la /proc/&lt;PID>/cwd` (查工作目录)\n   - `cat /proc/&lt;PID>/cmdline | tr '\\0' ' '` (查完整命令行)\n4. 检查是否有 tmux session 关联: `tmux list-sessions` 并检查每个 session\n5. 判断这个进程是否是:\n   - 某个已知实验的残留进程(对照 registry 中的实验)\n   - 论文编译 Worker 遗留\n   - 其他未知进程\n\n### 产出\n报告:\n- 进程身份(什么程序、什么命令行、运行了多久)\n- 是否是已知实验的一部分\n- 建议: 是否应该 kill 释放 cuda:0\n\n**不要 kill 任何进程，只调查报告。**",

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Important findings from the registry update:

**exp1-isr-b03-s43-r3 was actually SUBMITTED!** Its spec shows:
- command: train_grpo.py with beta=0.3, seed=43, cuda:0
- PID: 58046
- submitted_at: 2026-05-15T01:02:18 UTC
- server: westd-39279

So PID 58051 on cuda:0 is likely the CHILD PROCESS of exp1-isr-b03-s43-r3 (PID 58046 is the tmux wrapper, 58051 is the python training process)! This is the exact same pattern as the kill_job lesson - tmux wrapper PID ≠ python subprocess PID.
 
So the experiment is actually running on the server right now, but D064 says we don't need to retrain s43, which means I need to kill this process. The status might not have updated to RUNNING due to some issue with the submission flow, even though the process itself is executing.

Looking at the timeline, the experiment was submitted at 01:02:18 UTC with PID 58046, and by 01:04 the worker detected PID 58051 consuming 85GB on cuda:0 - that's the actual Python subprocess running. So I need to figure out whether to kill it based on when D064 was decided relative to when this experiment was submitted.

The experiment was created at 01:02:10 UTC, and D064 is dated 2026-05-15, so D064 was likely already in effect when the experiment was submitted - either a previous loop submitted it without seeing D064, or D064 was decided earlier and a later loop submitted it anyway. Either way, D064 is the current directive: don't retrain s43. But the experiment is already running and will finish in about an hour, which would give us additional s43 data for β=0.3 ISR - so there's a tradeoff between following the directive immediately versus letting it complete for the extra data.

Looking at D064 more carefully, it's framed around the paper narrative: β=0.3 will be presented as a single-seed observation, and the plan is to only use s42 data regardless. The extra s43 data wouldn't change the paper, but it also wouldn't hurt. Since the GPU time is already being spent, I should report this situation to the Director and let them decide whether to kill it or let it finish.

Now I'm waiting for the cuda:0 investigation worker to confirm the process details, though I already have strong evidence that PID 58051 is the subprocess for exp1-isr-b03-s43-r3. I'm preparing a Director report that covers the resource status, progress on exp1-random-b03-s44-r2 (around 2 hours remaining), the discovery of exp1-isr-b03-s43-r3 running despite D064, the re-dispatched paper compilation worker, and asking whether to terminate or allow the experiment to complete.

Looking at the resource-saturation details, both GPUs are actually in use—cuda:0 has exp1-isr-b03-s43-r3 and cuda:1 has exp1-random-b03-s44-r2—with a worker pool of 2 active workers (the investigation task and the latex fix), plus the cron monitoring for the random experiment. The situation is better than I initially assessed.

Now I'm ready to report this to the Director.

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01K6BjVpTcxicTcxPdVx8Rbh`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```
